PURPOSE
-------
Fourteen queued relay-agent prompts. The first three (001 / 002 /
003) were drafted to fire after the CT v1.3 release verdict
landed; the next four (004 / 005 / 006 / 007) were the
post-CT-v13 strategic moves driven by 20260502_picture.docx;
the math-closure batch (009 / 010 / 012 / 013) was driven by
strategic-picture v1.4 (residuals from 005 + 007 + 002);
the post-006 retry (014) closes G5 / G16 via a 13-parameter
ansatz refit on the saved CSVs; the post-009 follow-up (015)
closes G15 fully once operator acquires Okamoto 1987 + Conte-
Musette ch. 7 (via the existing G3b ILL/AMS workflow); and
the latest entry (016) is the post-010 PARTIAL refit-only
follow-up that extracts the second alien amplitude S_2 from
010's cached series and tests whether S_2 (or the canonical
ratio S_2/S_1²) discriminates the PCF-1 §3 sign-of-Δ_b
dichotomy at the second resurgent order.

Slot 008 is reserved for T1 Phase 2 (T1-PHASE-2-BT-APPLY) but
NOT YET DRAFTED — gated on operator primary-source
acquisition + Theory-Fleet H1 label arbitration.

Slot 011 is reserved for PCF1-V13-RECONCILE but NOT YET DRAFTED
— gated on operator option-(a)/(b) decision (see Prompt 002
HALT verdict).

017c_t37_s2_extraction_polynomial_aware.txt   ~1170 lines / ~30–45 min agent
    Post-016 HALT methodology fix v2 (rubber-duck blocking-issue
    fixes 2026-05-02 ~22:30 JST): TWO-STAGE WINDOWED FIT (NOT
    a single-window joint fit) on T_n := a_n·ζ_*^n/Γ(n).
    STAGE 1 fits T_n ≈ Σ_k α_k·n^{-k} on HIGH-n window
    [800, 1900] where 2^{-n} is below the noise floor; recovers
    C = α_0, a_k = α_k/α_0.
    STAGE 2 fits the next-rung on the polynomial-subtracted
    residual on LOWER-n window [40, 100] where 2^{-n} is still
    measurable AND lead-truncation is ≥100x below next-rung
    signal; recovers D = β_0, b_k = β_k/β_0.
    Basis: {1/n^k} with QR/SVD orthogonalization on the design
    matrix (NOT Chebyshev in n — v1's basis bug; the polynomial
    is in u=1/n not n). Linear identifiable parameterization.
    B.5 stability envelopes (K_lead × K_next × Stage1-window ×
    Stage2-window = 108 fits per rep) replace OLS sigma; gating
    uses median + half-range.
    B.6 free-β next-sector diagnostic catches non-zero β_2

  CMB.txt SHA  723E9C60543E08B574BB4BB7CA6E9407875C4A65100CD30C83CD206699EBC3B0
              (was 74073D94..68A5DC; differs from pre-paste -> paste landed)

  Line count   1921 (was 1900; +21 net new lines)
  OQ-token     1 hit (expected 1; uniqueness PASS)
  Header       1 hit (expected 1; uniqueness PASS)

THE HALT IS A SPEC DEFECT, NOT A CONTENT DEFECT:

  My 059 spec STEP 3 asserted `if ($lc -ne 1922) throw HALT_059_POST_LINE_COUNT`.
  But explicit NEW_STR rendering produces 24 lines and OLD_STR is 3 lines, so net
  delta = 21, not 22. File post-paste IS 1921 (correctly), and the assertion
  is wrong by one. The headline "22 new lines" in §STEP 2 was my drafting
  error; even my own structure breakdown
  ("1 blank + ===== + header + ===== + 1 blank + 14-line body + 1 blank + closing =====")
  sums to 21, not 22 (and the body is 13 lines not 14). The executer
  correctly halted on the asserted check + surfaced the spec defect.

EXECUTER ANOMALIES (4):
  D1 BLOCKING: spec arithmetic off-by-one (root cause of halt).
  D2 NON-BLOCKING (corrected in-session as J1): spec OLD_STR claimed
     L1899 has 2 trailing spaces ('  '); byte inspection showed L1899 is
     bare empty line (bytes 66 0A 0A 3D). STEP 1 anchors checked L1898 +
     L1900 only, missed L1899. Executer's edit-tool would have failed
     with 'old_str not found' if used spec verbatim; corrected OLD_STR
     to actual file content as judgment call.
  D3 NON-BLOCKING (cosmetic): spec body claimed 14 lines, actual 13
     (1 OQ-token + 12 prose lines).
  D4 INFORMATIONAL: spec STEP 4 'AEAL-A2 ~85549 bytes ± paste-bytes'
     phrasing loose; actual 87292 (delta +1743) consistent with the
     paste-block size.

DISPOSITION RECOMMENDATION: **OPT_A** (accept 1921 + amend spec
post-hoc). Reasoning:
  - Content is bit-correct vs source-of-truth substrate
    cmb_open_question_entry.txt at sessions/2026-05-06/048R-EARLY-FIRE-DECISION-SUBSTRATE/.
  - Only the assertion was wrong; the explicit NEW_STR rendering was
    authoritative and produces 1921 lines.
  - OPT_B (manual +1 line) would corrupt the section: no natural
    insertion point.
  - OPT_C (revert + re-fire) is wasteful: ~1700 bytes of correct
    content reverted only because of an arithmetic miscount on a
    metric I miscomputed.

EXECUTER AEAL: 6 claims (5 spec-listed 059-A1..A5 + 1 halt-state
059-A6). 4 anomalies surfaced. Zero unexpected_finds.

  (per prompt_file_naming convention; HALT suffix indicates non-clean

LESSONS LEARNED (memory-class):
  L1. Spec arithmetic must be verified by literal NEW_STR-line-count
      AT DRAFT TIME (not by structure-summary headcount). Future
      paste-class prompts: include a comment line in spec like
      `# DRAFT-TIME VERIFY: NEW_STR contains <N> lines; OLD_STR <M>;
      net delta = <N-M>; expected post-line-count = pre + (N-M) = <X>.`
  L2. STEP 1 anchor checks should cover EVERY line of the OLD_STR,
      not just sentinel lines (start/end). For 3-line OLD_STR, check
      all 3. For larger OLD_STR, hash the entire substring.
      Alternative: feed OLD_STR through a byte-equality assertion
      against the file's own substring at the anchor position before
      attempting the edit.
